Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 191 ===
"Who remember Allah while standing or sitting or lying on their sides and give thought to the creation of the heavens and the earth, saying, 'Our Lord, You did not create this aimlessly; exalted are You; so protect us from the punishment of the Fire.'"

Ulu al-albab described: they remember Allah standing, sitting, and on their sides — all postures of the day and night — and think about creation, then say: Rabbana ma khalaqta hadha batilan — You did not create this without purpose — subhanak, so protect us from the Fire. Dhikr + fikr + tawhid of purpose + fear of Fire. The complete cycle of a living mind and heart.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, remembered Allah in all states and taught adhkar for morning, evening, sleep, and waking. He said the example of the one who remembers and the one who does not is like the living and the dead. Reflection on creation was his night work with these ayat. Batilan — aimless — is refused; purpose fills the cosmos; therefore Fire is a just moral end for those who live as if there were no purpose.

Stand, sit, lie — no posture without dhikr. Think — no sky without reflection. Speak — no reflection without du'a. Protect us from the Fire — no theology without fear that cleans. Copy the four.

When modern voices say the universe is aimless, answer with this ayah's speech. Subhanak — high above aimlessness. Then ask for protection from Fire, because purpose implies accountability.

Build a habit stack: after every salah, one look at creation or one thought of the sky's order, then the sentence ma khalaqta hadha batilan. Ulu al-albab are made by repetition, not by one inspirational clip.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 192 ===
"Our Lord, indeed whoever You admit to the Fire — You have disgraced him, and for the wrongdoers there are no helpers."

The du'a continues: whoever You enter into the Fire, You have shamed him; zalimun have no helpers. Disgrace of the Fire is the true disgrace; dunya shame is small beside it. No helpers — the refrain of isolation for wrongdoers — returns at the edge of the surah's closing prayers.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, sought refuge from the disgrace of dunya and akhirah and taught fear of a Day when no soul helps another without permission. He described the shame of exposure of sins for some and the covering of Allah for others. Fire as disgrace redefines what you fear when people embarrass you for your deen.

No helpers for zalimun — do not be one, and do not rely on being one of a pack of them. Packs dissolve at the Fire's edge. Ask not to be entered into it; that is the core of this clause.

When humiliated for Islam in dunya, say: the real disgrace is the Fire. When tempted to zulm to avoid dunya shame, say: no helpers there. Both sentences free you.

Rabbana — keep the address soft and needy. You admit to the Fire whom You will with justice; we ask to be kept out by mercy. Disgrace us not with that entry. Helpers we need are You alone.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 193 ===
"Our Lord, indeed we have heard a caller calling to faith, saying, 'Believe in your Lord,' and we have believed. Our Lord, so forgive us our sins and remove from us our misdeeds and cause us to die with the righteous."

We heard a caller — the Prophet — calling to iman: believe in your Lord — and we believed. So forgive our sins, expiate our evil deeds, and take our souls with the abrar. Hearing, believing, then asking for maghfirah, kaffarah of sayyi'at, and death in the company of the righteous. The full path of a convert of heart in any age.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, was the caller, and he said the best of people are those who live long and whose deeds are good, and he taught asking for death upon islam and with the righteous. He said you will be with whom you love. Dying with the abrar is a destination that requires living toward them.

We heard and we believed — keep the ears open to the call still, through Quran and sunnah. Forgive, remove, take us with the righteous — three requests after iman. Iman without them is unfinished speech.

Make this du'a after every reminder that moves you. Hearing a khutbah and leaving without Rabbana is incomplete. The caller still calls in every recitation of Quran. Answer again.

Tawaffana ma'a al-abrar — write it in your phone's night list. Live the friendship of the righteous so that death finds you in their meaning if not always in their physical company. Company of the wicked is a bad place to die; this du'a refuses that ending.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 194 ===
"Our Lord, and give us what You promised us through Your messengers and do not disgrace us on the Day of Resurrection. Indeed, You do not fail in Your promise."

Give us what You promised us 'ala rusulik — through Your messengers — and do not disgrace us on the Day of Rising. You do not break the mi'ad. The du'a asks for the promised jannah and protection from disgrace, sealed with trust in His truthfulness. Promise of Allah is the rock under the whole surah's hope and fear.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, said Allah never breaks His promise, and he taught asking for the good the messengers promised. He described the disgrace of the Day for some faces and the honour for others. Do not disgrace us is a comprehensive ask covering public exposure of sin and the shame of the Fire.

What was promised through messengers: gardens, rivers, ridwan, seeing Him for the people of Paradise as authentic texts indicate, safety, honour. Ask for the whole package. He does not fail the promise; we fail the conditions — so ask for help with the conditions too.

Trust in the mi'ad kills despair. He does not fail. Your job is to stay on the side the promise was made for — the believers who do salihat and hold the rope. Promise plus path; not promise alone.

End hard days with this ayah. Give us what You promised. Do not disgrace us. You never fail. Then sleep as a person who has a covenant with the Truthful, not as a person who has only news and anxiety.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 195 ===
"And their Lord responded to them, 'Never will I allow to be lost the work of any worker among you, whether male or female; you are of one another. So those who emigrated or were evicted from their homes or were harmed in My cause or fought or were killed — I will surely remove from them their misdeeds, and I will surely admit them to gardens beneath which rivers flow, as reward from Allah, and Allah with Him is the best of reward.'"

Ijabah — He answered them. I do not waste work of a worker among you from male or female; some of you are from some. Those who made hijrah, were expelled, hurt in My path, fought, were killed — I will certainly expiate their evils and enter them into gardens with rivers: thawab from Allah, and with Allah is the best thawab. The answer is collective and gender-inclusive, lists forms of sacrifice, and promises kaffarah and jannah. Best of rewards is with Him — beyond even the described gardens.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, said women are the twins of men in the deen's address, and he took bay'ah from women, and he said the most complete believers are the best in character. He lived hijrah and expulsion and harm and fighting. This ayah is among the most comprehensive answers to the du'as of the ulu al-albab and the fighters together.

Male or female — no waste of either's work. Of one another — shared struggle. Hijrah, expulsion, harm, fight, death — five forms. Expiation then gardens — two gifts. Best reward with Allah — an open ceiling of fadl.

If your work is unseen because you are a woman in a home or a man without rank, read "I do not waste." If you were expelled or harmed for the path, read your name into the list. Best thawab is with Him — do not demand full payment in applause.

He answered them — so answer His call. The conversation of du'a and response is alive. Work; He does not waste. Sacrifice; He expiates and admits. Hope for the best of rewards with Him, not the best of trends with people.

=== 196 ===
"Be not deceived by the free movement of the disbelievers throughout the land."

Do not let the taqallub — free turning, movement, success — of those who disbelieve in the land deceive you. Their travel, trade, and power are not proof of truth. Short ayah after long promise: do not take their mobility as a theological argument.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, saw the wealth of Quraysh and the poverty of many companions and still taught that the world is a prison for the believer and a paradise for the disbeliever relative to their endings. He said if the world weighed a mosquito's wing with Allah He would not have given a kafir a sip. Movement in the land can be istidraj.

When their tourism, armies, and markets dazzle, recite this. La yaghurrannaka — let it not deceive you. Deception is the risk; the ayah forbids falling for it. Look at endings (3:137) and at the next ayah's brief enjoyment.

Practical: limit the envy-scroll of their glossy freedom if it harms your sabr. Replace with Quran's travel of reflection. Their turning in the land ends in a tight space if they die on kufr — the Fire as mathwa.

Believers may also move in the land for trade and da'wah; the ban is on deception by the disbelievers' movement as a criterion of truth, not on travel itself. Criterion is revelation. Keep it.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 197 ===
"It is a brief enjoyment; then their refuge is Hell, and wretched is the resting place."

Mata'un qalil — brief enjoyment — then Jahannam is their ma'wa, and bi'sa al-mihad, wretched the bed. The free movement of the disbelievers through the land (3:196) ends here in a short pleasure and a bad resting place. Qalil kills the illusion of long success. A bed should comfort; this one will not.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, said the dunya is cursed except what is of Allah in it, and he passed by a dead goat of little value to show how insignificant the world is with Allah compared to what people chase. Brief is the right adjective for every palace that does not end in His pleasure.

When their enjoyment looks long, stretch your timeline past the grave. Brief becomes obvious. Wretched bed becomes the true real estate headline. Do not buy their bed with your deen. Brief enjoyment is a bad trade for eternal rest.

Ma'wa and mihad — refuge and bed — both fail them. Your refuge should be Allah (3:150) and your provision for the barzakh should be deeds sent ahead. Prepare that mattress with salah, sadaqah, and tawbah before the brief enjoyment ends.

Hold this ayah with 196 and 198 as one panel: do not be deceived by their movement; it is brief; the muttaqin get gardens as lodging from Allah. Envy dies when the three are read together. The market of ghurur sells long pleasure; revelation marks it qalil and points to the wretched bed at the end of the receipt.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 198 ===
"But those who fear their Lord will have gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding eternally therein, as accommodation from Allah. And what is with Allah is best for the righteous."

Opposite real estate: muttaqin get gardens with rivers, forever, nuzulan — a hospitality lodging — from Allah. What is with Allah is better for the abrar. Accommodation from Him is honour language: guests of the King. Better than brief enjoyment is the stable with Allah.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, described the tents and rivers and spouses of Paradise and said the best is the pleasure of Allah. He called the abrar those who are good with Allah and with people. Nuzul is what you prepare for a guest — here Allah prepares it for those who feared Him.

Fear their Lord — past of taqwa that built the house. Forever — no eviction. What is with Allah is better — the scale that ends envy of 196-197. Abrar take the better.

Choose your lodging: brief mata' or nuzul from Allah. The price of the second is taqwa now. Pay. The rivers are not metaphorical in the creed of ahl al-sunnah; believe them as He willed and work.

When you host guests well in dunya, remember the Host who prepares nuzul for the muttaqin. Be His guest by fearing Him. Better is with Him for the righteous — make that your real estate motto.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 199 ===
"And indeed, among the People of the Book are those who believe in Allah and what was revealed to you and what was revealed to them, humble before Allah. They do not exchange the verses of Allah for a small price. Those will have their reward with their Lord. Indeed, Allah is swift in account."

Fairness at the end: among the People of the Book are those who believe in Allah, in what was sent to you, and in what was sent to them, khashi'in before Allah, not selling ayat for a small price. They have ajr with their Lord; He is sari' al-hisab. The surah that debated them hard still ends a thread with this honour for the sincere among them who accept the whole chain including the final Book. Humility and refusal of the small-price sale are their marks.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, stood for the janazah of a Jewish man in one report of respect for humanity, and he welcomed true belief from the People of the Book as a return to their own covenant. Najashi of Abyssinia is among those remembered with good. Swift account means their reward is not delayed by our disputes.

Marks to copy: belief in the whole revelation, khushu', not selling ayat. Reward with their Lord — safe storage. Swift account — hope and fear.

Do not erase this ayah when angry at crimes of other factions of the People of the Book. The Quran keeps the split to the last. And do not use it to erase the duty of believing in Muhammad — the ayah includes what was revealed to you.

Swift in account — end of the long surah's many deeds and debates. He counts fast and fully. Send what you want counted. The sincere of any background who meet this description find their ajr with Him; be among the sincere of your own background first.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.

=== 200 ===
"O you who believe, be patient, endure and remain stationed, and fear Allah that you may succeed."

The surah's final order to believers: isbiru, sabiru, rabitu, and ittaqu Allaha la'allakum tuflihun. Four: patience, mutual endurance (outlasting the enemy in sabr), ribat — staying firm at posts / guarding the frontiers of deen — and taqwa, so you may succeed. Uhud's whole lesson compresses into this line. Falah is the fruit of the four together.

The Prophet, peace be upon him, said wait for the prayer, for that is ribat, and he praised guarding the frontier. He said sabr is light and the strong is the one who controls himself. He ended lives of companions on these verbs. Mutual sabr — sabiru — means help each other endure; the ummah is a joint project of patience.

Be patient in the personal test; outlast in the long struggle; stay at your post of salah, of truth, of community duty; fear Allah in all of it. Success is not the world's applause; it is falah as Allah defines it — safety, Paradise, rope held.

This is the seal of Al Imran: after Isa, after Uhud, after the People of the Book, after the du'as of those of understanding — stand, endure, guard, fear. Do not close the mushaf and lose the four verbs. Put them on the wall of the house and of the heart.

La'allakum tuflihun — perhaps you will succeed. The perhaps of hope under Allah's will is enough to move the feet. Begin now: one act of sabr, one call to a brother to endure with you, one post you stop abandoning, one taqwa choice in private. The longest surah of debate ends on the shortest list of survival. Keep it.

The surah closes with hearing of ugly speech against Allah, the certainty of death, tests in wealth and selves, hurtful tongues, the covenant to clarify scripture, cosmic signs for those of understanding, and the long Rabbana of people who remember Allah in every posture. He answered them: no work of male or female is wasted; hijrah, harm, and struggle are met with expiation and gardens.

Do not be deceived by the free movement of disbelievers in the land; it is brief; Hell is a wretched bed; the muttaqin have lodging from Allah. Among the People of the Book are humble believers who do not sell ayat. Final order: patient, outlast, remain stationed, fear Allah — that you may succeed. Keep the four verbs after you close the mushaf.
